        .gc-edge { max-width:none !important; width:100%; padding-left:2rem !important; padding-right:2rem !important; margin-left:0; margin-right:0; }
        @media(max-width:575px){ .gc-edge { padding-left:1.1rem !important; padding-right:1.1rem !important; } }
        .gc-fip-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.65rem 0; }
        .gc-sig { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.65rem; text-decoration:none; color:#fff; }
        .gc-sig:hover { color:#fff; text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-sig img { height:30px; width:auto; }
        .gc-sig .brand-name { font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.05rem; letter-spacing:0; }
        .gc-sig .brand-sep { color:rgba(255,255,255,.32); font-weight:300; }
        .gc-sig .brand-product { font-weight:500; font-size:.9rem; opacity:.86; }
        .brand-portal-link {
          color:rgba(255,255,255,.78);
          font-size:.85rem;
          text-decoration:none;
          border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.24);
          border-radius:6px;
          padding:.28rem .75rem;
        }
        .brand-portal-link:hover { color:#fff; background:rgba(255,255,255,.08); }
    
        /* ── App bar ── */
        .gc-app-bar { background:#fff; border-bottom:3px solid transparent; border-image:var(--cs-grad) 1; }
        .gc-app-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.5rem 0; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-app-title {
          font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.2rem;
          color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.5rem;
        }
        .gc-app-title:hover,.gc-app-title:visited { color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-app-title .brand-icon { color:var(--cs-brand); font-size:1.4rem; }
    
        .gc-app-nav { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0; list-style:none; margin:0; padding:0; flex-wrap:wrap; }
        .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span {
          display:block; padding:.5rem .75rem; font-size:.9rem; font-weight:500;
          color:var(--cs-link); text-decoration:none; border-bottom:3px solid transparent;
          transition:border-color .2s, background .2s;
        }
        .gc-app-nav li a:visited { color:var(--cs-link); }
        .gc-app-nav li a:hover { background:#f1f5f9; border-bottom-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-bottom-color:var(--cs-brand); font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-menu { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-item:hover { background:#f1f5f9; }
    
        .gc-menu-btn { display:none; background:none; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:.3rem .6rem; cursor:pointer; font-size:1rem; color:var(--cs-text); }
        @media(max-width:991px) {
          .gc-menu-btn { display:block; }
          .gc-app-nav { display:none; width:100%; flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch; }
          .gc-app-nav li { width:100%; }
          .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span { text-align:left; }
          .gc-app-nav.show { display:flex; }
          .gc-app-nav li a { border-bottom:none; border-left:3px solid transparent; }
          .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-left-color:var(--cs-brand); border-bottom:none; }
        }
    
        /* ── Main ── */
        .gc-main { flex:1; padding:1.5rem 0; }
    
        /* ── Vanguard footer ── */
        .gc-footer-main { background:var(--cs-nav); color:rgba(255,255,255,.8); padding:1.25rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-main a { color:#93c5fd; }
        .gc-footer-main a:hover { color:#fff; }
        .gc-footer-links { list-style:none; padding:0; margin:0 0 .75rem; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.25rem 1.5rem; }
        .gc-footer-links a { font-size:.875rem; }
        .gc-footer-sub { background:#f1f5f9; padding:.75rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-sub-inner { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-modified { font-size:.8125rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        /* ═══ App components ════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        .card { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:14px; box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.06); overflow:hidden; }
        .card-header { background:var(--cs-nav); color:#fff; font-weight:600; padding:.9rem 1.25rem; border-bottom:none; border-radius:0; }
        .card-header.bg-light { background:var(--cs-light)!important; color:var(--cs-text); }
        /* Bootstrap's pale "-subtle" backgrounds need dark text — the default white above
           is unreadable on them. Covers every subtle variant, not just the ones in use. */
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] { color:var(--cs-text)!important; }
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] .badge.bg-dark { color:#fff!important; }
    
        /* Page header treatment (title + actions) used across admin views */
        .page-head h1 { letter-spacing:-.015em; }
        .page-head .rule { height:3px; width:56px; background:var(--cs-grad); border-radius:2px; margin-top:.5rem; }
    
        .btn { border-radius:9px; }
        .btn-primary { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); font-weight:600; }
        .btn-primary:hover,.btn-primary:focus { background:var(--cs-accent-dk); border-color:var(--cs-accent-dk); }
        .btn-lg { border-radius:11px; }
        .btn-outline-primary { color:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-outline-primary:hover { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-dark { background:var(--cs-nav); border-color:var(--cs-nav); }
    
        .form-control,.form-select { border-radius:6px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .form-control:focus,.form-select:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
        .form-label { font-weight:500; color:var(--cs-text); margin-bottom:.3rem; }
    
        .form-section {
          background:#fff; border-radius:6px; padding:1.5rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-left:4px solid var(--cs-accent);
        }
        .section-header { margin-bottom:1.5rem; padding-bottom:1rem; border-bottom:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .section-title { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.75rem; margin:0; font-size:1.2rem; }
        .section-number { background:var(--cs-accent); color:#fff; min-width:30px; height:30px; border-radius:50%; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:.75rem; font-weight:600; }
        .section-description { color:var(--cs-muted); margin-top:.5rem; font-size:.9375rem; }
    
        .progress { height:8px; border-radius:6px; }
        .progress-bar { background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--cs-accent),var(--cs-info)); border-radius:6px; }
    
        .stat-card {
          position:relative; background:#fff; border-radius:14px; padding:1.35rem 1.25rem; text-align:center;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.05);
          transition:box-shadow .2s, transform .2s; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .stat-card::before { content:""; position:absolute; top:0; left:0; right:0; height:3px; background:var(--cs-grad); opacity:.9; }
        .stat-card:hover { box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(15,32,54,.12); transform:translateY(-2px); }
        .stat-card .stat-icon { width:48px; height:48px; border-radius:12px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:1.5rem; margin:0 auto .75rem; }
        .stat-card .stat-number {
          font-size:2.1rem; font-weight:750; line-height:1; margin-bottom:.25rem;
          background:var(--cs-grad); -webkit-background-clip:text; background-clip:text; color:transparent;
        }
        .stat-card .stat-label { font-size:.85rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .alert { border:none; border-radius:6px; }
        .alert-success { background:#dcfce7; color:#14532d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-success); }
        .alert-danger { background:#fef2f2; color:#7f1d1d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-danger); }
        .alert-warning { background:#fffbeb; color:#78350f; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-warning); }
        .alert-info { background:#ecfeff; color:#164e63; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); }
    
        .table thead th { background:var(--cs-light); border-bottom:2px solid var(--cs-border); font-weight:600; font-size:.8125rem; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.5px; color:var(--cs-muted); }
        .table td { vertical-align:middle; }
    
        .badge { font-weight:500; padding:.35rem .65rem; border-radius:4px; }
        .badge.bg-success { background:var(--cs-success)!important; }
        .badge.bg-warning { background:var(--cs-warning)!important; color:#fff!important; }
        .badge.bg-danger { background:var(--cs-danger)!important; }
        .badge.bg-info { background:var(--cs-accent)!important; }
    
        .control-card { background:#fff; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1.25rem; margin-bottom:1rem; transition:border-color .2s; }
        .control-card:hover { border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .control-card.inherited { border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); background:#f0f9ff; }
        .control-card .control-id { font-family:monospace; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .control-card .control-title { font-weight:600; margin-bottom:.25rem; }
        .control-card .control-desc { font-size:.875rem; color:var(--gc-muted); }
    
        .comment-thread { max-height:300px; overflow-y:auto; }
        .comment-item { padding:.5rem; border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0; font-size:.875rem; }
        .comment-item:last-child { border-bottom:none; }
        .comment-author { font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .comment-time { font-size:.75rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .evidence-editor { min-height:150px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1rem; background:#fff; }
        .evidence-editor:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); outline:none; box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
    
        .progress-sticky { position:sticky; top:0; z-index:100; background:#fff; padding:1rem; border-radius:0 0 6px 6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); margin-bottom:1.5rem; }
    
        .audit-btn { width:40px; height:40px; border-radius:50%; border:2px solid; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; cursor:pointer; transition:all .2s; background:transparent; }
        .audit-btn.met { border-color:var(--cs-success); color:var(--cs-success); }
        .audit-btn.met.active { background:var(--cs-success); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.partial { border-color:var(--cs-warning); color:var(--cs-warning); }
        .audit-btn.partial.active { background:var(--cs-warning); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.not-met { border-color:var(--cs-danger); color:var(--cs-danger); }
        .audit-btn.not-met.active { background:var(--cs-danger); color:#fff; }
    
        @media(max-width:768px) {
          .gc-main { padding:1rem 0; }
          .form-section { padding:1rem; }
          .stat-card .stat-number { font-size:1.5rem; }
        }
        @media print {
          .gc-fip,.gc-app-bar,.gc-footer-main,.gc-footer-sub,.btn,.progress-sticky,.gc-skip { display:none!important; }
          .form-section { break-inside:avoid; box-shadow:none; border:1px solid #ddd; }
          body { background:#fff; }
        }
      </style>
      
    </head>

  actual: |-
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html class="no-js" lang="en" dir="ltr">
    <head>
      <meta charset="utf-8">
      <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
      <title>Projects - Aegis SA Platform</title>
      <meta name="description" content="Vanguard Cloud Services security assessment and authorization platform for multi-framework compliance, evidence collection, ATO/iATO, POA&M, and reporting workflows.">
      <meta name="dcterms.title" content="Aegis SA Platform">
      <meta name="dcterms.language" title="ISO639-2/T" content="eng">
      <meta name="dcterms.creator" content="Vanguard Cloud Services Inc.">
      <meta name="theme-color" content="#1a2332">
    
      <!-- Vanguard app fonts -->
      <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" crossorigin>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&family=Lato:wght@300;400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Bootstrap 5 + Icons (app internals) -->
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.2/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.11.1/font/bootstrap-icons.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Vanguard design system (shared visual language — layered on top of Bootstrap) -->
      <link href="/css/vanguard-design.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Dockable navigation + breadcrumb -->
      <link href="/css/nav-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Record action toolbar + draggable floating buttons -->
      <link href="/css/record-actions.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="/css/fab-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <style>
        /* ═══ Vanguard Cloud Services Design System — Aegis SA Platform ════════════ */
        :root {
          /* Aligned to the Vanguard product-brief palette (navy-950 + sky gradient) */
          --cs-nav:#0a1626; --cs-nav-hover:#0d2136;
          --cs-link:#2f80cf; --cs-link-hover:#1d4ed8; --cs-visited:#6d7fca;
          --cs-blue:#143453; --cs-brand:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-white:#fff; --cs-light:#f4f9fd; --cs-border:#dce7f2;
          --cs-text:#17273a; --cs-muted:#5f7185;
          --cs-success:#2fa06d; --cs-warning:#e0a53d; --cs-danger:#d64550; --cs-info:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-accent:#4d9fe0; --cs-accent-dk:#2f80cf;
          --cs-grad:linear-gradient(90deg,#6d7fca 0%,#4d9fe0 50%,#7cc9ee 100%);
    
          /* Backward-compatible aliases for existing templates */
          --gc-nav:var(--cs-nav); --gc-nav-hover:var(--cs-nav-hover);
          --gc-link:var(--cs-link); --gc-link-hover:var(--cs-link-hover); --gc-visited:var(--cs-visited);
          --gc-blue:var(--cs-blue); --gc-red:var(--cs-brand);
          --gc-white:var(--cs-white); --gc-light:var(--cs-light); --gc-border:var(--cs-border);
          --gc-text:var(--cs-text); --gc-muted:var(--cs-muted);
          --gc-success:var(--cs-success); --gc-warning:var(--cs-warning); --gc-danger:var(--cs-danger); --gc-info:var(--cs-info);
          --gc-accent:var(--cs-accent); --gc-accent-dk:var(--cs-accent-dk);
        }
        body {
          font-family:'Inter','Lato',sans-serif; font-size:1rem; line-height:1.65;
          color:var(--cs-text); background:var(--cs-light); margin:0;
          min-height:100vh; display:flex; flex-direction:column;
        }
        h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6 { font-family:'Lato','Inter',sans-serif; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-blue); line-height:1.2; }
        a { color:var(--cs-link); }
        a:hover { color:var(--cs-link-hover); }
        a:focus { outline:3px solid #fbbf24; outline-offset:0; }
    
        /* ── Skip links (WCAG 2.4.1) ── */
        .gc-skip {
          position:absolute; width:1px; height:1px; overflow:hidden;
          clip:rect(0,0,0,0); white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .gc-skip:focus {
          position:fixed; top:0; left:0; z-index:9999;
          width:auto; height:auto; clip:auto;
          padding:.75rem 1.5rem; background:#fbbf24; color:#000;
          font-weight:700; text-decoration:none; outline:3px solid #000;
        }
    
        /* ── Vanguard brand bar ── */
        .gc-fip { background:var(--cs-nav); }
        /* Full-width bar container: pulls brand/actions to the page edges,
           consistently for header and footer (overrides .container centering). */
        .gc-edge { max-width:none !important; width:100%; padding-left:2rem !important; padding-right:2rem !important; margin-left:0; margin-right:0; }
        @media(max-width:575px){ .gc-edge { padding-left:1.1rem !important; padding-right:1.1rem !important; } }
        .gc-fip-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.65rem 0; }
        .gc-sig { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.65rem; text-decoration:none; color:#fff; }
        .gc-sig:hover { color:#fff; text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-sig img { height:30px; width:auto; }
        .gc-sig .brand-name { font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.05rem; letter-spacing:0; }
        .gc-sig .brand-sep { color:rgba(255,255,255,.32); font-weight:300; }
        .gc-sig .brand-product { font-weight:500; font-size:.9rem; opacity:.86; }
        .brand-portal-link {
          color:rgba(255,255,255,.78);
          font-size:.85rem;
          text-decoration:none;
          border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.24);
          border-radius:6px;
          padding:.28rem .75rem;
        }
        .brand-portal-link:hover { color:#fff; background:rgba(255,255,255,.08); }
    
        /* ── App bar ── */
        .gc-app-bar { background:#fff; border-bottom:3px solid transparent; border-image:var(--cs-grad) 1; }
        .gc-app-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.5rem 0; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-app-title {
          font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.2rem;
          color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.5rem;
        }
        .gc-app-title:hover,.gc-app-title:visited { color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-app-title .brand-icon { color:var(--cs-brand); font-size:1.4rem; }
    
        .gc-app-nav { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0; list-style:none; margin:0; padding:0; flex-wrap:wrap; }
        .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span {
          display:block; padding:.5rem .75rem; font-size:.9rem; font-weight:500;
          color:var(--cs-link); text-decoration:none; border-bottom:3px solid transparent;
          transition:border-color .2s, background .2s;
        }
        .gc-app-nav li a:visited { color:var(--cs-link); }
        .gc-app-nav li a:hover { background:#f1f5f9; border-bottom-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-bottom-color:var(--cs-brand); font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-menu { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-item:hover { background:#f1f5f9; }
    
        .gc-menu-btn { display:none; background:none; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:.3rem .6rem; cursor:pointer; font-size:1rem; color:var(--cs-text); }
        @media(max-width:991px) {
          .gc-menu-btn { display:block; }
          .gc-app-nav { display:none; width:100%; flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch; }
          .gc-app-nav li { width:100%; }
          .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span { text-align:left; }
          .gc-app-nav.show { display:flex; }
          .gc-app-nav li a { border-bottom:none; border-left:3px solid transparent; }
          .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-left-color:var(--cs-brand); border-bottom:none; }
        }
    
        /* ── Main ── */
        .gc-main { flex:1; padding:1.5rem 0; }
    
        /* ── Vanguard footer ── */
        .gc-footer-main { background:var(--cs-nav); color:rgba(255,255,255,.8); padding:1.25rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-main a { color:#93c5fd; }
        .gc-footer-main a:hover { color:#fff; }
        .gc-footer-links { list-style:none; padding:0; margin:0 0 .75rem; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.25rem 1.5rem; }
        .gc-footer-links a { font-size:.875rem; }
        .gc-footer-sub { background:#f1f5f9; padding:.75rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-sub-inner { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-modified { font-size:.8125rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        /* ═══ App components ════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        .card { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:14px; box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.06); overflow:hidden; }
        .card-header { background:var(--cs-nav); color:#fff; font-weight:600; padding:.9rem 1.25rem; border-bottom:none; border-radius:0; }
        .card-header.bg-light { background:var(--cs-light)!important; color:var(--cs-text); }
        /* Bootstrap's pale "-subtle" backgrounds need dark text — the default white above
           is unreadable on them. Covers every subtle variant, not just the ones in use. */
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] { color:var(--cs-text)!important; }
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] .badge.bg-dark { color:#fff!important; }
    
        /* Page header treatment (title + actions) used across admin views */
        .page-head h1 { letter-spacing:-.015em; }
        .page-head .rule { height:3px; width:56px; background:var(--cs-grad); border-radius:2px; margin-top:.5rem; }
    
        .btn { border-radius:9px; }
        .btn-primary { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); font-weight:600; }
        .btn-primary:hover,.btn-primary:focus { background:var(--cs-accent-dk); border-color:var(--cs-accent-dk); }
        .btn-lg { border-radius:11px; }
        .btn-outline-primary { color:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-outline-primary:hover { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-dark { background:var(--cs-nav); border-color:var(--cs-nav); }
    
        .form-control,.form-select { border-radius:6px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .form-control:focus,.form-select:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
        .form-label { font-weight:500; color:var(--cs-text); margin-bottom:.3rem; }
    
        .form-section {
          background:#fff; border-radius:6px; padding:1.5rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-left:4px solid var(--cs-accent);
        }
        .section-header { margin-bottom:1.5rem; padding-bottom:1rem; border-bottom:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .section-title { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.75rem; margin:0; font-size:1.2rem; }
        .section-number { background:var(--cs-accent); color:#fff; min-width:30px; height:30px; border-radius:50%; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:.75rem; font-weight:600; }
        .section-description { color:var(--cs-muted); margin-top:.5rem; font-size:.9375rem; }
    
        .progress { height:8px; border-radius:6px; }
        .progress-bar { background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--cs-accent),var(--cs-info)); border-radius:6px; }
    
        .stat-card {
          position:relative; background:#fff; border-radius:14px; padding:1.35rem 1.25rem; text-align:center;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.05);
          transition:box-shadow .2s, transform .2s; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .stat-card::before { content:""; position:absolute; top:0; left:0; right:0; height:3px; background:var(--cs-grad); opacity:.9; }
        .stat-card:hover { box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(15,32,54,.12); transform:translateY(-2px); }
        .stat-card .stat-icon { width:48px; height:48px; border-radius:12px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:1.5rem; margin:0 auto .75rem; }
        .stat-card .stat-number {
          font-size:2.1rem; font-weight:750; line-height:1; margin-bottom:.25rem;
          background:var(--cs-grad); -webkit-background-clip:text; background-clip:text; color:transparent;
        }
        .stat-card .stat-label { font-size:.85rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .alert { border:none; border-radius:6px; }
        .alert-success { background:#dcfce7; color:#14532d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-success); }
        .alert-danger { background:#fef2f2; color:#7f1d1d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-danger); }
        .alert-warning { background:#fffbeb; color:#78350f; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-warning); }
        .alert-info { background:#ecfeff; color:#164e63; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); }
    
        .table thead th { background:var(--cs-light); border-bottom:2px solid var(--cs-border); font-weight:600; font-size:.8125rem; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.5px; color:var(--cs-muted); }
        .table td { vertical-align:middle; }
    
        .badge { font-weight:500; padding:.35rem .65rem; border-radius:4px; }
        .badge.bg-success { background:var(--cs-success)!important; }
        .badge.bg-warning { background:var(--cs-warning)!important; color:#fff!important; }
        .badge.bg-danger { background:var(--cs-danger)!important; }
        .badge.bg-info { background:var(--cs-accent)!important; }
    
        .control-card { background:#fff; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1.25rem; margin-bottom:1rem; transition:border-color .2s; }
        .control-card:hover { border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .control-card.inherited { border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); background:#f0f9ff; }
        .control-card .control-id { font-family:monospace; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .control-card .control-title { font-weight:600; margin-bottom:.25rem; }
        .control-card .control-desc { font-size:.875rem; color:var(--gc-muted); }
    
        .comment-thread { max-height:300px; overflow-y:auto; }
        .comment-item { padding:.5rem; border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0; font-size:.875rem; }
        .comment-item:last-child { border-bottom:none; }
        .comment-author { font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .comment-time { font-size:.75rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .evidence-editor { min-height:150px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1rem; background:#fff; }
        .evidence-editor:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); outline:none; box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
    
        .progress-sticky { position:sticky; top:0; z-index:100; background:#fff; padding:1rem; border-radius:0 0 6px 6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); margin-bottom:1.5rem; }
    
        .audit-btn { width:40px; height:40px; border-radius:50%; border:2px solid; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; cursor:pointer; transition:all .2s; background:transparent; }
        .audit-btn.met { border-color:var(--cs-success); color:var(--cs-success); }
        .audit-btn.met.active { background:var(--cs-success); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.partial { border-color:var(--cs-warning); color:var(--cs-warning); }
        .audit-btn.partial.active { background:var(--cs-warning); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.not-met { border-color:var(--cs-danger); color:var(--cs-danger); }
        .audit-btn.not-met.active { background:var(--cs-danger); color:#fff; }
    
        @media(max-width:768px) {
          .gc-main { padding:1rem 0; }
          .form-section { padding:1rem; }
          .stat-card .stat-number { font-size:1.5rem; }
        }
        @media print {
          .gc-fip,.gc-app-bar,.gc-footer-main,.gc-footer-sub,.btn,.progress-sticky,.gc-skip { display:none!important; }
          .form-section { break-inside:avoid; box-shadow:none; border:1px solid #ddd; }
          body { background:#fff; }
        }
      </style>

  code: 'ERR_ASSERTION'
  name: 'AssertionError'
  expected:
  actual: |-
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html class="no-js" lang="en" dir="ltr">
    <head>
      <meta charset="utf-8">
      <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
      <title>Projects - Aegis SA Platform</title>
      <meta name="description" content="Vanguard Cloud Services security assessment and authorization platform for multi-framework compliance, evidence collection, ATO/iATO, POA&M, and reporting workflows.">
      <meta name="dcterms.title" content="Aegis SA Platform">
      <meta name="dcterms.language" title="ISO639-2/T" content="eng">
      <meta name="dcterms.creator" content="Vanguard Cloud Services Inc.">
      <meta name="theme-color" content="#1a2332">
    
      <!-- Vanguard app fonts -->
      <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" crossorigin>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&family=Lato:wght@300;400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Bootstrap 5 + Icons (app internals) -->
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.2/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.11.1/font/bootstrap-icons.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Vanguard design system (shared visual language — layered on top of Bootstrap) -->
      <link href="/css/vanguard-design.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Dockable navigation + breadcrumb -->
      <link href="/css/nav-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Record action toolbar + draggable floating buttons -->
      <link href="/css/record-actions.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="/css/fab-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <style>
        /* ═══ Vanguard Cloud Services Design System — Aegis SA Platform ════════════ */
        :root {
          /* Aligned to the Vanguard product-brief palette (navy-950 + sky gradient) */
          --cs-nav:#0a1626; --cs-nav-hover:#0d2136;
          --cs-link:#2f80cf; --cs-link-hover:#1d4ed8; --cs-visited:#6d7fca;
          --cs-blue:#143453; --cs-brand:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-white:#fff; --cs-light:#f4f9fd; --cs-border:#dce7f2;
          --cs-text:#17273a; --cs-muted:#5f7185;
          --cs-success:#2fa06d; --cs-warning:#e0a53d; --cs-danger:#d64550; --cs-info:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-accent:#4d9fe0; --cs-accent-dk:#2f80cf;
          --cs-grad:linear-gradient(90deg,#6d7fca 0%,#4d9fe0 50%,#7cc9ee 100%);
    
          /* Backward-compatible aliases for existing templates */
          --gc-nav:var(--cs-nav); --gc-nav-hover:var(--cs-nav-hover);
          --gc-link:var(--cs-link); --gc-link-hover:var(--cs-link-hover); --gc-visited:var(--cs-visited);
          --gc-blue:var(--cs-blue); --gc-red:var(--cs-brand);
          --gc-white:var(--cs-white); --gc-light:var(--cs-light); --gc-border:var(--cs-border);
          --gc-text:var(--cs-text); --gc-muted:var(--cs-muted);
          --gc-success:var(--cs-success); --gc-warning:var(--cs-warning); --gc-danger:var(--cs-danger); --gc-info:var(--cs-info);
          --gc-accent:var(--cs-accent); --gc-accent-dk:var(--cs-accent-dk);
        }
        body {
          font-family:'Inter','Lato',sans-serif; font-size:1rem; line-height:1.65;
          color:var(--cs-text); background:var(--cs-light); margin:0;
          min-height:100vh; display:flex; flex-direction:column;
        }
        h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6 { font-family:'Lato','Inter',sans-serif; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-blue); line-height:1.2; }
        a { color:var(--cs-link); }
        a:hover { color:var(--cs-link-hover); }
        a:focus { outline:3px solid #fbbf24; outline-offset:0; }
    
        /* ── Skip links (WCAG 2.4.1) ── */
        .gc-skip {
          position:absolute; width:1px; height:1px; overflow:hidden;
          clip:rect(0,0,0,0); white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .gc-skip:focus {
          position:fixed; top:0; left:0; z-index:9999;
          width:auto; height:auto; clip:auto;
          padding:.75rem 1.5rem; background:#fbbf24; color:#000;
          font-weight:700; text-decoration:none; outline:3px solid #000;
        }
    
        /* ── Vanguard brand bar ── */
        .gc-fip { background:var(--cs-nav); }
        /* Full-width bar container: pulls brand/actions to the page edges,
           consistently for header and footer (overrides .container centering). */
        .gc-edge { max-width:none !important; width:100%; padding-left:2rem !important; padding-right:2rem !important; margin-left:0; margin-right:0; }
        @media(max-width:575px){ .gc-edge { padding-left:1.1rem !important; padding-right:1.1rem !important; } }
        .gc-fip-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.65rem 0; }
        .gc-sig { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.65rem; text-decoration:none; color:#fff; }
        .gc-sig:hover { color:#fff; text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-sig img { height:30px; width:auto; }
        .gc-sig .brand-name { font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.05rem; letter-spacing:0; }
        .gc-sig .brand-sep { color:rgba(255,255,255,.32); font-weight:300; }
        .gc-sig .brand-product { font-weight:500; font-size:.9rem; opacity:.86; }
        .brand-portal-link {
          color:rgba(255,255,255,.78);
          font-size:.85rem;
          text-decoration:none;
          border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.24);
          border-radius:6px;
          padding:.28rem .75rem;
        }
        .brand-portal-link:hover { color:#fff; background:rgba(255,255,255,.08); }
    
        /* ── App bar ── */
        .gc-app-bar { background:#fff; border-bottom:3px solid transparent; border-image:var(--cs-grad) 1; }
        .gc-app-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.5rem 0; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-app-title {
          font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.2rem;
          color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.5rem;
        }
        .gc-app-title:hover,.gc-app-title:visited { color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-app-title .brand-icon { color:var(--cs-brand); font-size:1.4rem; }
    
        .gc-app-nav { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0; list-style:none; margin:0; padding:0; flex-wrap:wrap; }
        .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span {
          display:block; padding:.5rem .75rem; font-size:.9rem; font-weight:500;
          color:var(--cs-link); text-decoration:none; border-bottom:3px solid transparent;
          transition:border-color .2s, background .2s;
        }
        .gc-app-nav li a:visited { color:var(--cs-link); }
        .gc-app-nav li a:hover { background:#f1f5f9; border-bottom-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-bottom-color:var(--cs-brand); font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-menu { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-item:hover { background:#f1f5f9; }
    
        .gc-menu-btn { display:none; background:none; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:.3rem .6rem; cursor:pointer; font-size:1rem; color:var(--cs-text); }
        @media(max-width:991px) {
          .gc-menu-btn { display:block; }
          .gc-app-nav { display:none; width:100%; flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch; }
          .gc-app-nav li { width:100%; }
          .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span { text-align:left; }
          .gc-app-nav.show { display:flex; }
          .gc-app-nav li a { border-bottom:none; border-left:3px solid transparent; }
          .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-left-color:var(--cs-brand); border-bottom:none; }
        }
    
        /* ── Main ── */
        .gc-main { flex:1; padding:1.5rem 0; }
    
        /* ── Vanguard footer ── */
        .gc-footer-main { background:var(--cs-nav); color:rgba(255,255,255,.8); padding:1.25rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-main a { color:#93c5fd; }
        .gc-footer-main a:hover { color:#fff; }
        .gc-footer-links { list-style:none; padding:0; margin:0 0 .75rem; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.25rem 1.5rem; }
        .gc-footer-links a { font-size:.875rem; }
        .gc-footer-sub { background:#f1f5f9; padding:.75rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-sub-inner { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-modified { font-size:.8125rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        /* ═══ App components ════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        .card { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:14px; box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.06); overflow:hidden; }
        .card-header { background:var(--cs-nav); color:#fff; font-weight:600; padding:.9rem 1.25rem; border-bottom:none; border-radius:0; }
        .card-header.bg-light { background:var(--cs-light)!important; color:var(--cs-text); }
        /* Bootstrap's pale "-subtle" backgrounds need dark text — the default white above
           is unreadable on them. Covers every subtle variant, not just the ones in use. */
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] { color:var(--cs-text)!important; }
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] .badge.bg-dark { color:#fff!important; }
    
        /* Page header treatment (title + actions) used across admin views */
        .page-head h1 { letter-spacing:-.015em; }
        .page-head .rule { height:3px; width:56px; background:var(--cs-grad); border-radius:2px; margin-top:.5rem; }
    
        .btn { border-radius:9px; }
        .btn-primary { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); font-weight:600; }
        .btn-primary:hover,.btn-primary:focus { background:var(--cs-accent-dk); border-color:var(--cs-accent-dk); }
        .btn-lg { border-radius:11px; }
        .btn-outline-primary { color:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-outline-primary:hover { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-dark { background:var(--cs-nav); border-color:var(--cs-nav); }
    
        .form-control,.form-select { border-radius:6px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .form-control:focus,.form-select:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
        .form-label { font-weight:500; color:var(--cs-text); margin-bottom:.3rem; }
    
        .form-section {
          background:#fff; border-radius:6px; padding:1.5rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-left:4px solid var(--cs-accent);
        }
        .section-header { margin-bottom:1.5rem; padding-bottom:1rem; border-bottom:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .section-title { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.75rem; margin:0; font-size:1.2rem; }
        .section-number { background:var(--cs-accent); color:#fff; min-width:30px; height:30px; border-radius:50%; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:.75rem; font-weight:600; }
        .section-description { color:var(--cs-muted); margin-top:.5rem; font-size:.9375rem; }
    
        .progress { height:8px; border-radius:6px; }
        .progress-bar { background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--cs-accent),var(--cs-info)); border-radius:6px; }
    
        .stat-card {
          position:relative; background:#fff; border-radius:14px; padding:1.35rem 1.25rem; text-align:center;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.05);
          transition:box-shadow .2s, transform .2s; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .stat-card::before { content:""; position:absolute; top:0; left:0; right:0; height:3px; background:var(--cs-grad); opacity:.9; }
        .stat-card:hover { box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(15,32,54,.12); transform:translateY(-2px); }
        .stat-card .stat-icon { width:48px; height:48px; border-radius:12px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:1.5rem; margin:0 auto .75rem; }
        .stat-card .stat-number {
          font-size:2.1rem; font-weight:750; line-height:1; margin-bottom:.25rem;
          background:var(--cs-grad); -webkit-background-clip:text; background-clip:text; color:transparent;
        }
        .stat-card .stat-label { font-size:.85rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .alert { border:none; border-radius:6px; }
        .alert-success { background:#dcfce7; color:#14532d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-success); }
        .alert-danger { background:#fef2f2; color:#7f1d1d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-danger); }
        .alert-warning { background:#fffbeb; color:#78350f; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-warning); }
        .alert-info { background:#ecfeff; color:#164e63; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); }
    
        .table thead th { background:var(--cs-light); border-bottom:2px solid var(--cs-border); font-weight:600; font-size:.8125rem; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.5px; color:var(--cs-muted); }
        .table td { vertical-align:middle; }
    
        .badge { font-weight:500; padding:.35rem .65rem; border-radius:4px; }
        .badge.bg-success { background:var(--cs-success)!important; }
        .badge.bg-warning { background:var(--cs-warning)!important; color:#fff!important; }
        .badge.bg-danger { background:var(--cs-danger)!important; }
        .badge.bg-info { background:var(--cs-accent)!important; }
    
        .control-card { background:#fff; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1.25rem; margin-bottom:1rem; transition:border-color .2s; }
        .control-card:hover { border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .control-card.inherited { border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); background:#f0f9ff; }
        .control-card .control-id { font-family:monospace; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .control-card .control-title { font-weight:600; margin-bottom:.25rem; }
        .control-card .control-desc { font-size:.875rem; color:var(--gc-muted); }
    
        .comment-thread { max-height:300px; overflow-y:auto; }
        .comment-item { padding:.5rem; border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0; font-size:.875rem; }
        .comment-item:last-child { border-bottom:none; }
        .comment-author { font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .comment-time { font-size:.75rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .evidence-editor { min-height:150px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1rem; background:#fff; }
        .evidence-editor:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); outline:none; box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
    
        .progress-sticky { position:sticky; top:0; z-index:100; background:#fff; padding:1rem; border-radius:0 0 6px 6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); margin-bottom:1.5rem; }
    
        .audit-btn { width:40px; height:40px; border-radius:50%; border:2px solid; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; cursor:pointer; transition:all .2s; background:transparent; }
        .audit-btn.met { border-color:var(--cs-success); color:var(--cs-success); }
        .audit-btn.met.active { background:var(--cs-success); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.partial { border-color:var(--cs-warning); color:var(--cs-warning); }
        .audit-btn.partial.active { background:var(--cs-warning); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.not-met { border-color:var(--cs-danger); color:var(--cs-danger); }
        .audit-btn.not-met.active { background:var(--cs-danger); color:#fff; }
    
        @media(max-width:768px) {
          .gc-main { padding:1rem 0; }
          .form-section { padding:1rem; }
          .stat-card .stat-number { font-size:1.5rem; }
        }
        @media print {
          .gc-fip,.gc-app-bar,.gc-footer-main,.gc-footer-sub,.btn,.progress-sticky,.gc-skip { display:none!important; }
          .form-section { break-inside:avoid; box-shadow:none; border:1px solid #ddd; }
          body { background:#fff; }
        }

  actual: |-
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html class="no-js" lang="en" dir="ltr">
    <head>
      <meta charset="utf-8">
      <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
      <title>Assessments - Aegis SA Platform</title>
      <meta name="description" content="Vanguard Cloud Services security assessment and authorization platform for multi-framework compliance, evidence collection, ATO/iATO, POA&M, and reporting workflows.">
      <meta name="dcterms.title" content="Aegis SA Platform">
      <meta name="dcterms.language" title="ISO639-2/T" content="eng">
      <meta name="dcterms.creator" content="Vanguard Cloud Services Inc.">
      <meta name="theme-color" content="#1a2332">
    
      <!-- Vanguard app fonts -->
      <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" crossorigin>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&family=Lato:wght@300;400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Bootstrap 5 + Icons (app internals) -->
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.2/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.11.1/font/bootstrap-icons.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Vanguard design system (shared visual language — layered on top of Bootstrap) -->
      <link href="/css/vanguard-design.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Dockable navigation + breadcrumb -->
      <link href="/css/nav-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Record action toolbar + draggable floating buttons -->
      <link href="/css/record-actions.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="/css/fab-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <style>
        /* ═══ Vanguard Cloud Services Design System — Aegis SA Platform ════════════ */
        :root {
          /* Aligned to the Vanguard product-brief palette (navy-950 + sky gradient) */
          --cs-nav:#0a1626; --cs-nav-hover:#0d2136;
          --cs-link:#2f80cf; --cs-link-hover:#1d4ed8; --cs-visited:#6d7fca;
          --cs-blue:#143453; --cs-brand:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-white:#fff; --cs-light:#f4f9fd; --cs-border:#dce7f2;
          --cs-text:#17273a; --cs-muted:#5f7185;
          --cs-success:#2fa06d; --cs-warning:#e0a53d; --cs-danger:#d64550; --cs-info:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-accent:#4d9fe0; --cs-accent-dk:#2f80cf;
          --cs-grad:linear-gradient(90deg,#6d7fca 0%,#4d9fe0 50%,#7cc9ee 100%);
    
          /* Backward-compatible aliases for existing templates */
          --gc-nav:var(--cs-nav); --gc-nav-hover:var(--cs-nav-hover);
          --gc-link:var(--cs-link); --gc-link-hover:var(--cs-link-hover); --gc-visited:var(--cs-visited);
          --gc-blue:var(--cs-blue); --gc-red:var(--cs-brand);
          --gc-white:var(--cs-white); --gc-light:var(--cs-light); --gc-border:var(--cs-border);
          --gc-text:var(--cs-text); --gc-muted:var(--cs-muted);
          --gc-success:var(--cs-success); --gc-warning:var(--cs-warning); --gc-danger:var(--cs-danger); --gc-info:var(--cs-info);
          --gc-accent:var(--cs-accent); --gc-accent-dk:var(--cs-accent-dk);
        }
        body {
          font-family:'Inter','Lato',sans-serif; font-size:1rem; line-height:1.65;
          color:var(--cs-text); background:var(--cs-light); margin:0;
          min-height:100vh; display:flex; flex-direction:column;
        }
        h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6 { font-family:'Lato','Inter',sans-serif; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-blue); line-height:1.2; }
        a { color:var(--cs-link); }
        a:hover { color:var(--cs-link-hover); }
        a:focus { outline:3px solid #fbbf24; outline-offset:0; }
    
        /* ── Skip links (WCAG 2.4.1) ── */
        .gc-skip {
          position:absolute; width:1px; height:1px; overflow:hidden;
          clip:rect(0,0,0,0); white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .gc-skip:focus {
          position:fixed; top:0; left:0; z-index:9999;
          width:auto; height:auto; clip:auto;
          padding:.75rem 1.5rem; background:#fbbf24; color:#000;
          font-weight:700; text-decoration:none; outline:3px solid #000;
        }
    
        /* ── Vanguard brand bar ── */
        .gc-fip { background:var(--cs-nav); }
        /* Full-width bar container: pulls brand/actions to the page edges,
           consistently for header and footer (overrides .container centering). */
        .gc-edge { max-width:none !important; width:100%; padding-left:2rem !important; padding-right:2rem !important; margin-left:0; margin-right:0; }
        @media(max-width:575px){ .gc-edge { padding-left:1.1rem !important; padding-right:1.1rem !important; } }
        .gc-fip-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.65rem 0; }
        .gc-sig { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.65rem; text-decoration:none; color:#fff; }
        .gc-sig:hover { color:#fff; text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-sig img { height:30px; width:auto; }
        .gc-sig .brand-name { font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.05rem; letter-spacing:0; }
        .gc-sig .brand-sep { color:rgba(255,255,255,.32); font-weight:300; }
        .gc-sig .brand-product { font-weight:500; font-size:.9rem; opacity:.86; }
        .brand-portal-link {
          color:rgba(255,255,255,.78);
          font-size:.85rem;
          text-decoration:none;
          border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.24);
          border-radius:6px;
          padding:.28rem .75rem;
        }
        .brand-portal-link:hover { color:#fff; background:rgba(255,255,255,.08); }
    
        /* ── App bar ── */
        .gc-app-bar { background:#fff; border-bottom:3px solid transparent; border-image:var(--cs-grad) 1; }
        .gc-app-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.5rem 0; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-app-title {
          font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.2rem;
          color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.5rem;
        }
        .gc-app-title:hover,.gc-app-title:visited { color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-app-title .brand-icon { color:var(--cs-brand); font-size:1.4rem; }
    
        .gc-app-nav { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0; list-style:none; margin:0; padding:0; flex-wrap:wrap; }
        .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span {
          display:block; padding:.5rem .75rem; font-size:.9rem; font-weight:500;
          color:var(--cs-link); text-decoration:none; border-bottom:3px solid transparent;
          transition:border-color .2s, background .2s;
        }
        .gc-app-nav li a:visited { color:var(--cs-link); }
        .gc-app-nav li a:hover { background:#f1f5f9; border-bottom-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-bottom-color:var(--cs-brand); font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-menu { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-item:hover { background:#f1f5f9; }
    
        .gc-menu-btn { display:none; background:none; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:.3rem .6rem; cursor:pointer; font-size:1rem; color:var(--cs-text); }
        @media(max-width:991px) {
          .gc-menu-btn { display:block; }
          .gc-app-nav { display:none; width:100%; flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch; }
          .gc-app-nav li { width:100%; }
          .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span { text-align:left; }
          .gc-app-nav.show { display:flex; }
          .gc-app-nav li a { border-bottom:none; border-left:3px solid transparent; }
          .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-left-color:var(--cs-brand); border-bottom:none; }
        }
    
        /* ── Main ── */
        .gc-main { flex:1; padding:1.5rem 0; }
    
        /* ── Vanguard footer ── */
        .gc-footer-main { background:var(--cs-nav); color:rgba(255,255,255,.8); padding:1.25rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-main a { color:#93c5fd; }
        .gc-footer-main a:hover { color:#fff; }
        .gc-footer-links { list-style:none; padding:0; margin:0 0 .75rem; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.25rem 1.5rem; }
        .gc-footer-links a { font-size:.875rem; }
        .gc-footer-sub { background:#f1f5f9; padding:.75rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-sub-inner { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-modified { font-size:.8125rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        /* ═══ App components ════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        .card { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:14px; box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.06); overflow:hidden; }
        .card-header { background:var(--cs-nav); color:#fff; font-weight:600; padding:.9rem 1.25rem; border-bottom:none; border-radius:0; }
        .card-header.bg-light { background:var(--cs-light)!important; color:var(--cs-text); }
        /* Bootstrap's pale "-subtle" backgrounds need dark text — the default white above
           is unreadable on them. Covers every subtle variant, not just the ones in use. */
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] { color:var(--cs-text)!important; }
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] .badge.bg-dark { color:#fff!important; }
    
        /* Page header treatment (title + actions) used across admin views */
        .page-head h1 { letter-spacing:-.015em; }
        .page-head .rule { height:3px; width:56px; background:var(--cs-grad); border-radius:2px; margin-top:.5rem; }
    
        .btn { border-radius:9px; }
        .btn-primary { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); font-weight:600; }
        .btn-primary:hover,.btn-primary:focus { background:var(--cs-accent-dk); border-color:var(--cs-accent-dk); }
        .btn-lg { border-radius:11px; }
        .btn-outline-primary { color:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-outline-primary:hover { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-dark { background:var(--cs-nav); border-color:var(--cs-nav); }
    
        .form-control,.form-select { border-radius:6px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .form-control:focus,.form-select:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
        .form-label { font-weight:500; color:var(--cs-text); margin-bottom:.3rem; }
    
        .form-section {
          background:#fff; border-radius:6px; padding:1.5rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-left:4px solid var(--cs-accent);
        }
        .section-header { margin-bottom:1.5rem; padding-bottom:1rem; border-bottom:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .section-title { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.75rem; margin:0; font-size:1.2rem; }
        .section-number { background:var(--cs-accent); color:#fff; min-width:30px; height:30px; border-radius:50%; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:.75rem; font-weight:600; }
        .section-description { color:var(--cs-muted); margin-top:.5rem; font-size:.9375rem; }
    
        .progress { height:8px; border-radius:6px; }
        .progress-bar { background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--cs-accent),var(--cs-info)); border-radius:6px; }
    
        .stat-card {
          position:relative; background:#fff; border-radius:14px; padding:1.35rem 1.25rem; text-align:center;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.05);
          transition:box-shadow .2s, transform .2s; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .stat-card::before { content:""; position:absolute; top:0; left:0; right:0; height:3px; background:var(--cs-grad); opacity:.9; }
        .stat-card:hover { box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(15,32,54,.12); transform:translateY(-2px); }
        .stat-card .stat-icon { width:48px; height:48px; border-radius:12px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:1.5rem; margin:0 auto .75rem; }
        .stat-card .stat-number {
          font-size:2.1rem; font-weight:750; line-height:1; margin-bottom:.25rem;
          background:var(--cs-grad); -webkit-background-clip:text; background-clip:text; color:transparent;
        }
        .stat-card .stat-label { font-size:.85rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .alert { border:none; border-radius:6px; }
        .alert-success { background:#dcfce7; color:#14532d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-success); }
        .alert-danger { background:#fef2f2; color:#7f1d1d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-danger); }
        .alert-warning { background:#fffbeb; color:#78350f; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-warning); }
        .alert-info { background:#ecfeff; color:#164e63; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); }
    
        .table thead th { background:var(--cs-light); border-bottom:2px solid var(--cs-border); font-weight:600; font-size:.8125rem; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.5px; color:var(--cs-muted); }
        .table td { vertical-align:middle; }
    
        .badge { font-weight:500; padding:.35rem .65rem; border-radius:4px; }
        .badge.bg-success { background:var(--cs-success)!important; }
        .badge.bg-warning { background:var(--cs-warning)!important; color:#fff!important; }
        .badge.bg-danger { background:var(--cs-danger)!important; }
        .badge.bg-info { background:var(--cs-accent)!important; }
    
        .control-card { background:#fff; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1.25rem; margin-bottom:1rem; transition:border-color .2s; }
        .control-card:hover { border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .control-card.inherited { border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); background:#f0f9ff; }
        .control-card .control-id { font-family:monospace; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .control-card .control-title { font-weight:600; margin-bottom:.25rem; }
        .control-card .control-desc { font-size:.875rem; color:var(--gc-muted); }
    
        .comment-thread { max-height:300px; overflow-y:auto; }
        .comment-item { padding:.5rem; border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0; font-size:.875rem; }
        .comment-item:last-child { border-bottom:none; }
        .comment-author { font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .comment-time { font-size:.75rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .evidence-editor { min-height:150px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1rem; background:#fff; }
        .evidence-editor:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); outline:none; box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
    
        .progress-sticky { position:sticky; top:0; z-index:100; background:#fff; padding:1rem; border-radius:0 0 6px 6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); margin-bottom:1.5rem; }
    
        .audit-btn { width:40px; height:40px; border-radius:50%; border:2px solid; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; cursor:pointer; transition:all .2s; background:transparent; }
        .audit-btn.met { border-color:var(--cs-success); color:var(--cs-success); }
        .audit-btn.met.active { background:var(--cs-success); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.partial { border-color:var(--cs-warning); color:var(--cs-warning); }
        .audit-btn.partial.active { background:var(--cs-warning); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.not-met { border-color:var(--cs-danger); color:var(--cs-danger); }
        .audit-btn.not-met.active { background:var(--cs-danger); color:#fff; }
    
        @media(max-width:768px) {
          .gc-main { padding:1rem 0; }
          .form-section { padding:1rem; }
          .stat-card .stat-number { font-size:1.5rem; }
        }
        @media print {
          .gc-fip,.gc-app-bar,.gc-footer-main,.gc-footer-sub,.btn,.progress-sticky,.gc-skip { display:none!important; }
          .form-section { break-inside:avoid; box-shadow:none; border:1px solid #ddd; }
          body { background:#fff; }
        }
      </style>

    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html class="no-js" lang="en" dir="ltr">
    <head>
      <meta charset="utf-8">
      <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
      <title>Dashboard - Aegis SA Platform</title>
      <meta name="description" content="Vanguard Cloud Services security assessment and authorization platform for multi-framework compliance, evidence collection, ATO/iATO, POA&M, and reporting workflows.">
      <meta name="dcterms.title" content="Aegis SA Platform">
      <meta name="dcterms.language" title="ISO639-2/T" content="eng">
      <meta name="dcterms.creator" content="Vanguard Cloud Services Inc.">
      <meta name="theme-color" content="#1a2332">
    
      <!-- Vanguard app fonts -->
      <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" crossorigin>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&family=Lato:wght@300;400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Bootstrap 5 + Icons (app internals) -->
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.2/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.11.1/font/bootstrap-icons.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <!-- Vanguard design system (shared visual language — layered on top of Bootstrap) -->
      <link href="/css/vanguard-design.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Dockable navigation + breadcrumb -->
      <link href="/css/nav-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <!-- Record action toolbar + draggable floating buttons -->
      <link href="/css/record-actions.css" rel="stylesheet">
      <link href="/css/fab-dock.css" rel="stylesheet">
    
      <style>
        /* ═══ Vanguard Cloud Services Design System — Aegis SA Platform ════════════ */
        :root {
          /* Aligned to the Vanguard product-brief palette (navy-950 + sky gradient) */
          --cs-nav:#0a1626; --cs-nav-hover:#0d2136;
          --cs-link:#2f80cf; --cs-link-hover:#1d4ed8; --cs-visited:#6d7fca;
          --cs-blue:#143453; --cs-brand:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-white:#fff; --cs-light:#f4f9fd; --cs-border:#dce7f2;
          --cs-text:#17273a; --cs-muted:#5f7185;
          --cs-success:#2fa06d; --cs-warning:#e0a53d; --cs-danger:#d64550; --cs-info:#4d9fe0;
          --cs-accent:#4d9fe0; --cs-accent-dk:#2f80cf;
          --cs-grad:linear-gradient(90deg,#6d7fca 0%,#4d9fe0 50%,#7cc9ee 100%);
    
          /* Backward-compatible aliases for existing templates */
          --gc-nav:var(--cs-nav); --gc-nav-hover:var(--cs-nav-hover);
          --gc-link:var(--cs-link); --gc-link-hover:var(--cs-link-hover); --gc-visited:var(--cs-visited);
          --gc-blue:var(--cs-blue); --gc-red:var(--cs-brand);
          --gc-white:var(--cs-white); --gc-light:var(--cs-light); --gc-border:var(--cs-border);
          --gc-text:var(--cs-text); --gc-muted:var(--cs-muted);
          --gc-success:var(--cs-success); --gc-warning:var(--cs-warning); --gc-danger:var(--cs-danger); --gc-info:var(--cs-info);
          --gc-accent:var(--cs-accent); --gc-accent-dk:var(--cs-accent-dk);
        }
        body {
          font-family:'Inter','Lato',sans-serif; font-size:1rem; line-height:1.65;
          color:var(--cs-text); background:var(--cs-light); margin:0;
          min-height:100vh; display:flex; flex-direction:column;
        }
        h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6 { font-family:'Lato','Inter',sans-serif; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-blue); line-height:1.2; }
        a { color:var(--cs-link); }
        a:hover { color:var(--cs-link-hover); }
        a:focus { outline:3px solid #fbbf24; outline-offset:0; }
    
        /* ── Skip links (WCAG 2.4.1) ── */
        .gc-skip {
          position:absolute; width:1px; height:1px; overflow:hidden;
          clip:rect(0,0,0,0); white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .gc-skip:focus {
          position:fixed; top:0; left:0; z-index:9999;
          width:auto; height:auto; clip:auto;
          padding:.75rem 1.5rem; background:#fbbf24; color:#000;
          font-weight:700; text-decoration:none; outline:3px solid #000;
        }
    
        /* ── Vanguard brand bar ── */
        .gc-fip { background:var(--cs-nav); }
        /* Full-width bar container: pulls brand/actions to the page edges,
           consistently for header and footer (overrides .container centering). */
        .gc-edge { max-width:none !important; width:100%; padding-left:2rem !important; padding-right:2rem !important; margin-left:0; margin-right:0; }
        @media(max-width:575px){ .gc-edge { padding-left:1.1rem !important; padding-right:1.1rem !important; } }
        .gc-fip-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.65rem 0; }
        .gc-sig { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.65rem; text-decoration:none; color:#fff; }
        .gc-sig:hover { color:#fff; text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-sig img { height:30px; width:auto; }
        .gc-sig .brand-name { font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.05rem; letter-spacing:0; }
        .gc-sig .brand-sep { color:rgba(255,255,255,.32); font-weight:300; }
        .gc-sig .brand-product { font-weight:500; font-size:.9rem; opacity:.86; }
        .brand-portal-link {
          color:rgba(255,255,255,.78);
          font-size:.85rem;
          text-decoration:none;
          border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.24);
          border-radius:6px;
          padding:.28rem .75rem;
        }
        .brand-portal-link:hover { color:#fff; background:rgba(255,255,255,.08); }
    
        /* ── App bar ── */
        .gc-app-bar { background:#fff; border-bottom:3px solid transparent; border-image:var(--cs-grad) 1; }
        .gc-app-inner { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:.5rem 0; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-app-title {
          font-family:'Lato',sans-serif; font-weight:700; font-size:1.2rem;
          color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.5rem;
        }
        .gc-app-title:hover,.gc-app-title:visited { color:var(--cs-blue); text-decoration:none; }
        .gc-app-title .brand-icon { color:var(--cs-brand); font-size:1.4rem; }
    
        .gc-app-nav { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0; list-style:none; margin:0; padding:0; flex-wrap:wrap; }
        .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span {
          display:block; padding:.5rem .75rem; font-size:.9rem; font-weight:500;
          color:var(--cs-link); text-decoration:none; border-bottom:3px solid transparent;
          transition:border-color .2s, background .2s;
        }
        .gc-app-nav li a:visited { color:var(--cs-link); }
        .gc-app-nav li a:hover { background:#f1f5f9; border-bottom-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-bottom-color:var(--cs-brand); font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-menu { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); }
        .gc-app-nav .dropdown-item:hover { background:#f1f5f9; }
    
        .gc-menu-btn { display:none; background:none; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:.3rem .6rem; cursor:pointer; font-size:1rem; color:var(--cs-text); }
        @media(max-width:991px) {
          .gc-menu-btn { display:block; }
          .gc-app-nav { display:none; width:100%; flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch; }
          .gc-app-nav li { width:100%; }
          .gc-app-nav li a, .gc-app-nav li > span { text-align:left; }
          .gc-app-nav.show { display:flex; }
          .gc-app-nav li a { border-bottom:none; border-left:3px solid transparent; }
          .gc-app-nav li a.active { border-left-color:var(--cs-brand); border-bottom:none; }
        }
    
        /* ── Main ── */
        .gc-main { flex:1; padding:1.5rem 0; }
    
        /* ── Vanguard footer ── */
        .gc-footer-main { background:var(--cs-nav); color:rgba(255,255,255,.8); padding:1.25rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-main a { color:#93c5fd; }
        .gc-footer-main a:hover { color:#fff; }
        .gc-footer-links { list-style:none; padding:0; margin:0 0 .75rem; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.25rem 1.5rem; }
        .gc-footer-links a { font-size:.875rem; }
        .gc-footer-sub { background:#f1f5f9; padding:.75rem 0; }
        .gc-footer-sub-inner { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:.5rem; }
        .gc-modified { font-size:.8125rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        /* ═══ App components ════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        .card { border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:14px; box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.06); overflow:hidden; }
        .card-header { background:var(--cs-nav); color:#fff; font-weight:600; padding:.9rem 1.25rem; border-bottom:none; border-radius:0; }
        .card-header.bg-light { background:var(--cs-light)!important; color:var(--cs-text); }
        /* Bootstrap's pale "-subtle" backgrounds need dark text — the default white above
           is unreadable on them. Covers every subtle variant, not just the ones in use. */
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] { color:var(--cs-text)!important; }
        .card-header[class*="-subtle"] .badge.bg-dark { color:#fff!important; }
    
        /* Page header treatment (title + actions) used across admin views */
        .page-head h1 { letter-spacing:-.015em; }
        .page-head .rule { height:3px; width:56px; background:var(--cs-grad); border-radius:2px; margin-top:.5rem; }
    
        .btn { border-radius:9px; }
        .btn-primary { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); font-weight:600; }
        .btn-primary:hover,.btn-primary:focus { background:var(--cs-accent-dk); border-color:var(--cs-accent-dk); }
        .btn-lg { border-radius:11px; }
        .btn-outline-primary { color:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-outline-primary:hover { background:var(--cs-accent); border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .btn-dark { background:var(--cs-nav); border-color:var(--cs-nav); }
    
        .form-control,.form-select { border-radius:6px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .form-control:focus,.form-select:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
        .form-label { font-weight:500; color:var(--cs-text); margin-bottom:.3rem; }
    
        .form-section {
          background:#fff; border-radius:6px; padding:1.5rem; margin-bottom:1.5rem;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-left:4px solid var(--cs-accent);
        }
        .section-header { margin-bottom:1.5rem; padding-bottom:1rem; border-bottom:1px solid var(--cs-border); }
        .section-title { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:.75rem; margin:0; font-size:1.2rem; }
        .section-number { background:var(--cs-accent); color:#fff; min-width:30px; height:30px; border-radius:50%; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:.75rem; font-weight:600; }
        .section-description { color:var(--cs-muted); margin-top:.5rem; font-size:.9375rem; }
    
        .progress { height:8px; border-radius:6px; }
        .progress-bar { background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--cs-accent),var(--cs-info)); border-radius:6px; }
    
        .stat-card {
          position:relative; background:#fff; border-radius:14px; padding:1.35rem 1.25rem; text-align:center;
          border:1px solid var(--cs-border); box-shadow:0 2px 10px rgba(15,32,54,.05);
          transition:box-shadow .2s, transform .2s; overflow:hidden;
        }
        .stat-card::before { content:""; position:absolute; top:0; left:0; right:0; height:3px; background:var(--cs-grad); opacity:.9; }
        .stat-card:hover { box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(15,32,54,.12); transform:translateY(-2px); }
        .stat-card .stat-icon { width:48px; height:48px; border-radius:12px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; font-size:1.5rem; margin:0 auto .75rem; }
        .stat-card .stat-number {
          font-size:2.1rem; font-weight:750; line-height:1; margin-bottom:.25rem;
          background:var(--cs-grad); -webkit-background-clip:text; background-clip:text; color:transparent;
        }
        .stat-card .stat-label { font-size:.85rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .alert { border:none; border-radius:6px; }
        .alert-success { background:#dcfce7; color:#14532d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-success); }
        .alert-danger { background:#fef2f2; color:#7f1d1d; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-danger); }
        .alert-warning { background:#fffbeb; color:#78350f; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-warning); }
        .alert-info { background:#ecfeff; color:#164e63; border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); }
    
        .table thead th { background:var(--cs-light); border-bottom:2px solid var(--cs-border); font-weight:600; font-size:.8125rem; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.5px; color:var(--cs-muted); }
        .table td { vertical-align:middle; }
    
        .badge { font-weight:500; padding:.35rem .65rem; border-radius:4px; }
        .badge.bg-success { background:var(--cs-success)!important; }
        .badge.bg-warning { background:var(--cs-warning)!important; color:#fff!important; }
        .badge.bg-danger { background:var(--cs-danger)!important; }
        .badge.bg-info { background:var(--cs-accent)!important; }
    
        .control-card { background:#fff; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1.25rem; margin-bottom:1rem; transition:border-color .2s; }
        .control-card:hover { border-color:var(--cs-accent); }
        .control-card.inherited { border-left:4px solid var(--cs-info); background:#f0f9ff; }
        .control-card .control-id { font-family:monospace; font-weight:700; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .control-card .control-title { font-weight:600; margin-bottom:.25rem; }
        .control-card .control-desc { font-size:.875rem; color:var(--gc-muted); }
    
        .comment-thread { max-height:300px; overflow-y:auto; }
        .comment-item { padding:.5rem; border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0; font-size:.875rem; }
        .comment-item:last-child { border-bottom:none; }
        .comment-author { font-weight:600; color:var(--cs-nav); }
        .comment-time { font-size:.75rem; color:var(--cs-muted); }
    
        .evidence-editor { min-height:150px; border:1px solid var(--cs-border); border-radius:6px; padding:1rem; background:#fff; }
        .evidence-editor:focus { border-color:var(--cs-accent); outline:none; box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(37,99,235,.2); }
    
        .progress-sticky { position:sticky; top:0; z-index:100; background:#fff; padding:1rem; border-radius:0 0 6px 6px; box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1); margin-bottom:1.5rem; }
    
        .audit-btn { width:40px; height:40px; border-radius:50%; border:2px solid; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; cursor:pointer; transition:all .2s; background:transparent; }
        .audit-btn.met { border-color:var(--cs-success); color:var(--cs-success); }
        .audit-btn.met.active { background:var(--cs-success); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.partial { border-color:var(--cs-warning); color:var(--cs-warning); }
        .audit-btn.partial.active { background:var(--cs-warning); color:#fff; }
        .audit-btn.not-met { border-color:var(--cs-danger); color:var(--cs-danger); }
        .audit-btn.not-met.active { background:var(--cs-danger); color:#fff; }
    
        @media(max-width:768px) {
          .gc-main { padding:1rem 0; }
          .form-section { padding:1rem; }
          .stat-card .stat-number { font-size:1.5rem; }
        }
        @media print {
          .gc-fip,.gc-app-bar,.gc-footer-main,.gc-footer-sub,.btn,.progress-sticky,.gc-skip { display:none!important; }
          .form-section { break-inside:avoid; box-shadow:none; border:1px solid #ddd; }
          body { background:#fff; }
        }
      </style>
      
    </head>

      <main class="gc-main" id="main-content" role="main" property="mainContentOfPage">

      <!-- Stats Cards (each navigates to its list) -->

            </div>

        </div>

              <div class="stat-label">Active Assessments</div>
            </div>

        </div>

              <div class="stat-label">Pending Audits</div>
            </div>

        </div>
        <div class="col-6 col-lg">

              <div class="stat-number">0</div>
              <div class="stat-label">Active ATOs</div>
            </div>

        </div>
        <div class="col-6 col-lg">

      <!-- AI token usage (built-in Anthropic allowance) -->
          <div class="card mb-4">

              <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center flex-wrap gap-2 mb-2">
                <div><i class="bi bi-robot me-2" style="color:var(--cs-accent)"></i><strong>Built-in AI usage</strong> <span class="text-muted small">this month</span></div>
                <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-2">
                  <span class="small text-muted">0 / 250000 tokens (0%)</span>
                  
                </div>
              </div>

                <div class="progress-bar" role="progressbar" style="width:0%"></div>
              </div>

                250000 tokens remaining.

              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
    
      <div class="row g-4">
        <!-- Recent Projects -->
        <div class="col-lg-6">
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
              <span><i class="bi bi-folder2-open me-2"></i>Recent Projects</span>
              <a href="/admin/projects" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light">View All</a>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-0">
                <div class="p-4 text-center text-muted">
                  <i class="bi bi-folder-plus" style="font-size:2rem"></i>
                  <p class="mt-2 mb-0">No projects yet. <a href="/admin/projects/new">Create your first project</a></p>
                </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
    
        <!-- Recent Assessments -->
        <div class="col-lg-6">
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
              <span><i class="bi bi-clipboard-check me-2"></i>Recent Assessments</span>
              <a href="/admin/assessments" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light">View All</a>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-0">
                <div class="p-4 text-center text-muted">
                  <i class="bi bi-clipboard-plus" style="font-size:2rem"></i>
                  <p class="mt-2 mb-0">No assessments yet.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    
    
      <!-- Recent Intakes -->
      <div class="row mt-4">
        <div class="col-12">
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
              <span><i class="bi bi-inbox me-2"></i>Recent Intakes</span>
              <a href="/admin/intakes" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light">View All</a>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-0">
                <div class="p-4 text-center text-muted">
                  <i class="bi bi-inbox" style="font-size:2rem"></i>
                  <p class="mt-2 mb-0">No intakes submitted yet. Share the <a href="/intake" target="_blank">intake form</a> with project teams.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
